HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B004","root_000744/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_28e4f8da8ba04faf3843","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:7:2\",\"root_000744\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:7:6\",\"root_000278\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Daha sonraki deve, bu satırdaki yağlanmama ve cılız boşluğu su, otlak ve beden rezervlerinden oluşan bozulmuş bir hayvancılık düzenine bağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B004","root_000744/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2924a8908dccb4cf8820","text":"3. **Pastoral depletion before the camel sign.** Evidence: `س م ن` B001 animal fatness; `ج و ع` B004 emptiness/leanness; later `ء ب ل` B001 camels and `خ ش ع` B004 depleted hump. Structural cue: camel bodies appear later as objects to inspect. Role: the negated fatness anticipates visible animal morphology. Abductive move: the surah teaches through bodies whose stored fat or height is legible. Reading change: the hunger line prepares the reader to see moral consequence as bodily leanness and depleted elevation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B001","root_001110/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2db0e8592a8aaa2ce8d8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:7:4\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Zenginlik ve yeterlilik çağrışımı, önceki emeğin hiçbir kazanç ya da karşılık üretmediği ekonomik bir yoksunluk da ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_001110/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3169e02c6f37e02f8e72","text":"2. **Economic insufficiency after labor.** Evidence: `غ ن ي` B001 wealth/self-sufficiency, B002 availing; `ع م ل` B004 wage/pay in 88:3; later `ح س ب` B001 reckoning. Structural cue: labor precedes, account follows. Role: `يغني` asks whether the result pays off or avails; it does not. Abductive move: punishment is measured partly by the collapse of exchange: work yields no sufficiency. Reading change: the ayah becomes economic as well as bodily: no profit, no covering of need.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B001","root_000744/B001","root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_751ac9de4d813b63659e","text":"1. **Double negation of nourishment and sufficiency.** Evidence: `س م ن` B001 fatness; `غ ن ي` B002 sufficing/availing, B001 self-sufficiency; `ج و ع` B001 empty stomach. Structural cue: two coordinated negations follow the defective food of 88:6. Role: fatness is bodily surplus; ghina is functional sufficiency; hunger is the deficit that remains. Abductive move: true food must change both body-state and need-state. Reading change: the ayah reads as a diagnostic test of failed provision.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B004","root_001110/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_85fe2ffb000b8dd16391","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:7:4\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:7:6\",\"root_000278\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Açlığın boşluğu ile kendi kendine yetememe birleşince eksiklik yalnız midede değil, beden ile onu yaşatan çevre arasındaki ilişkide görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B001","root_000744/B001","root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_86b11f3d4482dd0350d5","text":"1. **The paired negation tests provision at two distinct scales.** **Lexical:** `س م ن` B001, bodily fatness; `غ ن ي` B002, sufficing or availing; and `ج و ع` B001, an empty stomach. **Cue:** parallel `لَا ... وَلَا` predicates move from measurable bodily gain to relief of the underlying need, both governed by the food of 88:6. **Role:** fatness is the somatic output metric, sufficiency is the functional output metric, and hunger is the unresolved input state. **Abduction:** I infer a staged evaluation rather than synonymous repetition. **Reading change:** the ayah becomes a compact failure analysis: the food produces neither stored bodily benefit nor even immediate need-resolution.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B001","root_000744/B001","root_001110/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_99ad76535dc1f21db37f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:7:2\",\"root_000744\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:7:4\",\"root_001110\",[\"B002\"]],[\"88:7:6\",\"root_000278\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İki olumsuzlama besini iki ayrı ölçekte sınar: Bedende yedek oluşturma ile mevcut ihtiyacı işlevsel olarak giderme birbirinden ayrılır ve ikisi de başarısız olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c6078d807c72a135e781","text":"88:8-12 unexpectedly expanded `غ ن ي` beyond immediate relief. Soft faces, satisfaction, garden, acoustic rest, and finally a running spring activated B004 settled dwelling, so the failed food was seen to block durable habitation as well as bodily satiety.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000278/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c9f169156b3069e5d5af","text":"2. **Hunger expands from an empty stomach into an empty provisioning relation.** **Lexical:** `ج و ع` B004, \"hunger as emptiness or leanness,\" and `غ ن ي` B001, self-sufficiency or wealth. **Cue:** the denial follows the exclusive plant ration of 88:6 and is then answered by ease, satisfaction, a garden, silence, and a flowing spring in 88:8-12. **Role:** the emptiness branch broadens lack beyond the belly, while the wealth branch names the absent capacity to sustain oneself. **Abduction:** I infer that the later environment exemplifies what sufficiency would require; it is not explicitly presented as a definition. **Reading change:** `من جوع` names not only a pang but a failed relation between body and world, one that the following integrated habitat reverses.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cd80e45e22b6e2d6faf8","text":"3. **Sufficiency includes the capacity to settle and remain, which this food cannot produce.** **Lexical:** `غ ن ي` B004, \"settled dwelling or having been there.\" **Cue:** `لَا يُغْنِى` is followed within the window by `فِى جَنَّةٍ عَالِيَةٍ`, acoustic rest, and a running spring in 88:10-12. **Role:** the dwelling branch supplies a durable spatial consequence of being sufficiently provisioned. **Abduction:** I infer that food which does not avail cannot establish inhabitable continuity, whereas the following garden can. **Reading change:** the failure is not just momentary nutrition; it is inability to support a settled life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e6debcb736a1f3550e96","text":"The later `الإبل` made this ayah’s animal-body vocabulary unavoidable: fatness, hump, pasture, and water form one ecological chain. The positive `ناعمة` faces sharpened the contrast between a body that cannot be made full and a face living in ease.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ff04af8d6b0e1a88d040","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:7:4\",\"root_001110\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yeterliliğin yerleşip kalmayı da kapsaması, bu yiyeceğin anlık doygunluk yanında sürdürülebilir bir yaşam alanı kuramadığını; sonraki bahçenin ise tam bunu sağladığını gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
